from __future__ import annotations

import json
import statistics
from pathlib import Path


ENGINE = "CAD3D_WINDOW_VERTICAL_MEASUREMENTS_V1"

MAIN_ENTRANCE_DOOR_HEIGHT_CM = 210.0
STOREY_CLEAR_HEIGHT_CM = 300.0
INTERFLOOR_THICKNESS_CM = 35.0
FLOOR_TO_FLOOR_CM = (
    STOREY_CLEAR_HEIGHT_CM
    + INTERFLOOR_THICKNESS_CM
)

GROUND_REFERENCE_RULE = (
    "MAIN_ENTRANCE_DOOR_BOTTOM_V1"
)


def _number(value, default=None):
    try:
        if value is None:
            return default
        return float(value)
    except Exception:
        return default


def _door_pairs(match):
    return [
        pair
        for pair in (
            match.get(
                "opening_pairs",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )
        if pair.get("kind") == "door"
    ]


def _window_pairs(match):
    return [
        pair
        for pair in (
            match.get(
                "opening_pairs",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )
        if pair.get("kind") == "window"
    ]


def _pair_bottom(pair):
    return _number(
        pair.get(
            "elevation_bottom_y"
        )
    )


def _pair_top(pair):
    return _number(
        pair.get(
            "elevation_top_y"
        )
    )


def _pair_height(pair):
    value = _number(
        pair.get(
            "elevation_height"
        )
    )

    if value is not None:
        return abs(value)

    bottom = _pair_bottom(pair)
    top = _pair_top(pair)

    if (
        bottom is None
        or top is None
    ):
        return None

    return abs(
        top - bottom
    )


def _plan_side_widths(analysis):
    result = {}

    for facade in (
        analysis.get(
            "plan_facades",
            [],
        )
        or []
    ):
        side = facade.get("side")

        width = _number(
            facade.get(
                "expected_width"
            )
        )

        if (
            side
            and width is not None
            and width > 0.0
        ):
            result[str(side)] = width

    return result


def _choose_main_entrance(
    analysis,
):
    matches = list(
        analysis.get(
            "matches",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    candidates = []

    all_widths = []

    for match in matches:
        for pair in _door_pairs(match):
            width = _number(
                pair.get(
                    "plan_width"
                )
            )

            if (
                width is not None
                and width > 0.0
            ):
                all_widths.append(
                    width
                )

    if not all_widths:
        return None

    median_width = statistics.median(
        all_widths
    )

    side_widths = _plan_side_widths(
        analysis
    )

    max_side_width = max(
        side_widths.values(),
        default=0.0,
    )

    for match_index, match in enumerate(
        matches
    ):
        doors = _door_pairs(
            match
        )

        if not doors:
            continue

        side = str(
            match.get(
                "plan_side",
                "",
            )
        )

        side_width = side_widths.get(
            side,
            0.0,
        )

        for door_index, pair in enumerate(
            doors
        ):
            width = _number(
                pair.get(
                    "plan_width"
                ),
                median_width,
            )

            if width is None:
                width = median_width

            width_ratio = (
                width / median_width
                if median_width > 1.0e-9
                else 1.0
            )

            # Main entrance preference:
            # 1) normal single-door width,
            # 2) facade with one external door,
            # 3) longer exterior facade as tie breaker.
            wide_penalty = (
                100.0
                if width_ratio > 1.55
                else 0.0
            )

            door_count_penalty = (
                abs(
                    len(doors) - 1
                )
                * 20.0
            )

            width_penalty = (
                abs(
                    width_ratio - 1.0
                )
                * 10.0
            )

            long_facade_bonus = 0.0

            if (
                max_side_width > 0.0
                and side_width > 0.0
            ):
                long_facade_bonus = (
                    side_width
                    / max_side_width
                ) * 2.0

            score = (
                wide_penalty
                + door_count_penalty
                + width_penalty
                - long_facade_bonus
            )

            candidates.append(
                (
                    score,
                    match_index,
                    door_index,
                    match,
                    pair,
                )
            )

    if not candidates:
        return None

    candidates.sort(
        key=lambda row: (
            row[0],
            str(
                row[3].get(
                    "plan_side",
                    "",
                )
            ),
            int(
                row[3].get(
                    "facade_number",
                    0,
                )
                or 0
            ),
        )
    )

    (
        score,
        match_index,
        door_index,
        match,
        pair,
    ) = candidates[0]

    bottom = _pair_bottom(
        pair
    )

    top = _pair_top(
        pair
    )

    height = _pair_height(
        pair
    )

    if (
        bottom is None
        or top is None
        or height is None
        or height <= 1.0e-9
    ):
        return None

    return {
        "score":
            float(score),

        "match_index":
            int(match_index),

        "door_index":
            int(door_index),

        "facade_number":
            match.get(
                "facade_number"
            ),

        "plan_side":
            match.get(
                "plan_side"
            ),

        "plan_width":
            _number(
                pair.get(
                    "plan_width"
                )
            ),

        "elevation_bottom_y":
            bottom,

        "elevation_top_y":
            top,

        "elevation_height":
            height,
    }


def _choose_local_registration_door(
    match,
    main_plan_width,
):
    doors = _door_pairs(
        match
    )

    if not doors:
        return None

    def rank(pair):
        width = _number(
            pair.get(
                "plan_width"
            )
        )

        if (
            width is None
            or main_plan_width is None
        ):
            width_error = 0.0
        else:
            width_error = abs(
                width - main_plan_width
            )

        bottom = _pair_bottom(
            pair
        )

        return (
            width_error,
            (
                bottom
                if bottom is not None
                else float("inf")
            ),
        )

    pair = min(
        doors,
        key=rank,
    )

    bottom = _pair_bottom(
        pair
    )

    if bottom is None:
        return None

    return pair


def calculate_window_vertical_measurements(
    analysis,
):
    result = {
        "engine":
            ENGINE,

        "ok":
            False,

        "ground_reference_rule":
            GROUND_REFERENCE_RULE,

        "storey_clear_height_cm":
            STOREY_CLEAR_HEIGHT_CM,

        "interfloor_thickness_cm":
            INTERFLOOR_THICKNESS_CM,

        "floor_to_floor_cm":
            FLOOR_TO_FLOOR_CM,

        "main_entrance":
            None,

        "cm_per_drawing_unit":
            None,

        "windows":
            [],

        "unresolved":
            [],
    }

    entrance = _choose_main_entrance(
        analysis
    )

    if entrance is None:
        result[
            "error"
        ] = (
            "MAIN_ENTRANCE_DOOR_NOT_RESOLVED"
        )

        return result

    entrance_height = float(
        entrance[
            "elevation_height"
        ]
    )

    # Vertical drawing scale is calibrated by the
    # architectural 210 cm entrance-door contract.
    cm_per_unit = (
        MAIN_ENTRANCE_DOOR_HEIGHT_CM
        / entrance_height
    )

    entrance = dict(
        entrance
    )

    entrance[
        "z_cm"
    ] = 0.0

    entrance[
        "known_height_cm"
    ] = (
        MAIN_ENTRANCE_DOOR_HEIGHT_CM
    )

    entrance[
        "reference_rule"
    ] = GROUND_REFERENCE_RULE

    result[
        "main_entrance"
    ] = entrance

    result[
        "cm_per_drawing_unit"
    ] = cm_per_unit

    matches = list(
        analysis.get(
            "matches",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    window_counter = 0

    for match_index, match in enumerate(
        matches
    ):
        facade_number = match.get(
            "facade_number"
        )

        plan_side = match.get(
            "plan_side"
        )

        windows = _window_pairs(
            match
        )

        if not windows:
            continue

        if (
            match_index
            == entrance["match_index"]
        ):
            local_reference_y = float(
                entrance[
                    "elevation_bottom_y"
                ]
            )

            registration_source = (
                "MAIN_ENTRANCE_DOOR_BOTTOM"
            )

        else:
            registration_door = (
                _choose_local_registration_door(
                    match,
                    entrance.get(
                        "plan_width"
                    ),
                )
            )

            if registration_door is None:
                for pair_index, pair in enumerate(
                    windows
                ):
                    result[
                        "unresolved"
                    ].append(
                        {
                            "facade_number":
                                facade_number,

                            "plan_side":
                                plan_side,

                            "plan_index":
                                pair.get(
                                    "plan_index"
                                ),

                            "reason":
                                (
                                    "NO_MATCHED_DOOR_FOR_LOCAL_"
                                    "ELEVATION_REGISTRATION"
                                ),
                        }
                    )

                continue

            local_reference_y = float(
                _pair_bottom(
                    registration_door
                )
            )

            # Important:
            # this does NOT redefine Z=0.
            # It only aligns a separately positioned
            # elevation drawing to the canonical datum
            # established by the main entrance door.
            registration_source = (
                "LOCAL_FACADE_REGISTERED_TO_"
                "MAIN_ENTRANCE_DATUM_BY_MATCHED_DOOR"
            )

        storey_index = int(
            match.get(
                "storey_index",
                analysis.get(
                    "storey_index",
                    0,
                ),
            )
            or 0
        )

        storey_base_cm = (
            storey_index
            * FLOOR_TO_FLOOR_CM
        )

        for pair in windows:
            bottom = _pair_bottom(
                pair
            )

            top = _pair_top(
                pair
            )

            height_units = _pair_height(
                pair
            )

            if (
                bottom is None
                or top is None
                or height_units is None
            ):
                result[
                    "unresolved"
                ].append(
                    {
                        "facade_number":
                            facade_number,

                        "plan_side":
                            plan_side,

                        "plan_index":
                            pair.get(
                                "plan_index"
                            ),

                        "reason":
                            "WINDOW_VERTICAL_GEOMETRY_MISSING",
                    }
                )

                continue

            window_counter += 1

            bottom_z_cm = (
                (
                    bottom
                    - local_reference_y
                )
                * cm_per_unit
                + storey_base_cm
            )

            height_cm = (
                height_units
                * cm_per_unit
            )

            top_z_cm = (
                bottom_z_cm
                + height_cm
            )

            result[
                "windows"
            ].append(
                {
                    "window_id":
                        f"W{window_counter:02d}",

                    "facade_number":
                        facade_number,

                    "plan_side":
                        plan_side,

                    "plan_index":
                        pair.get(
                            "plan_index"
                        ),

                    "elevation_index":
                        pair.get(
                            "elevation_index"
                        ),

                    "storey_index":
                        storey_index,

                    "drawing_bottom_y":
                        bottom,

                    "drawing_top_y":
                        top,

                    "drawing_height":
                        height_units,

                    "window_bottom_z_cm":
                        round(
                            bottom_z_cm,
                            2,
                        ),

                    "window_height_cm":
                        round(
                            height_cm,
                            2,
                        ),

                    "window_top_z_cm":
                        round(
                            top_z_cm,
                            2,
                        ),

                    "interfloor_thickness_cm":
                        INTERFLOOR_THICKNESS_CM,

                    "ground_reference_rule":
                        GROUND_REFERENCE_RULE,

                    "registration_source":
                        registration_source,
                }
            )

    result[
        "window_count"
    ] = len(
        result["windows"]
    )

    result[
        "unresolved_count"
    ] = len(
        result["unresolved"]
    )

    result[
        "ok"
    ] = (
        result["window_count"] > 0
    )

    return result


def _write_log(
    measurements,
):
    root = Path(
        __file__
    ).resolve().parents[2]

    log_dir = (
        root
        / "logs"
    )

    log_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    path = (
        log_dir
        / "window_vertical_measurements.json"
    )

    path.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            measurements,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    return path


def _print_report(
    measurements,
    log_path,
):
    print("")
    print(
        "=== WINDOW VERTICAL MEASUREMENTS ==="
    )

    print(
        "ENGINE :",
        measurements.get(
            "engine"
        ),
    )

    print(
        "Z=0    : MAIN ENTRANCE DOOR BOTTOM"
    )

    print(
        "SLAB   :",
        f"{INTERFLOOR_THICKNESS_CM:.2f} cm",
    )

    print(
        "FLOOR  :",
        f"{FLOOR_TO_FLOOR_CM:.2f} cm",
    )

    entrance = measurements.get(
        "main_entrance"
    )

    if entrance:
        print(
            "ENTRY  :",
            "facade=",
            entrance.get(
                "facade_number"
            ),
            "side=",
            entrance.get(
                "plan_side"
            ),
        )

    print(
        "SCALE  :",
        measurements.get(
            "cm_per_drawing_unit"
        ),
        "cm/unit",
    )

    for row in (
        measurements.get(
            "windows",
            [],
        )
        or []
    ):
        print(
            (
                f'{row["window_id"]} | '
                f'facade={row["facade_number"]} | '
                f'side={row["plan_side"]} | '
                f'sill={row["window_bottom_z_cm"]:.2f} cm | '
                f'height={row["window_height_cm"]:.2f} cm | '
                f'top={row["window_top_z_cm"]:.2f} cm'
            )
        )

    unresolved = measurements.get(
        "unresolved",
        [],
    ) or []

    if unresolved:
        print(
            "UNRESOLVED:",
            len(unresolved),
        )

        for row in unresolved:
            print(
                " -",
                row,
            )

    print(
        "LOG    :",
        str(log_path),
    )

    print(
        "=== END WINDOW VERTICAL MEASUREMENTS ==="
    )

    print("")


def attach_window_vertical_measurements(
    analysis,
):
    if not isinstance(
        analysis,
        dict,
    ):
        return analysis

    measurements = (
        calculate_window_vertical_measurements(
            analysis
        )
    )

    analysis[
        "window_vertical_measurements"
    ] = measurements

    log_path = _write_log(
        measurements
    )

    _print_report(
        measurements,
        log_path,
    )

    return analysis


def self_test():
    analysis = {
        "plan_facades": [
            {
                "side": "top",
                "expected_width": 10000.0,
            },
            {
                "side": "left",
                "expected_width": 6000.0,
            },
        ],

        "matches": [
            {
                "facade_number": 1,
                "plan_side": "top",
                "opening_pairs": [
                    {
                        "kind": "door",
                        "plan_index": 0,
                        "plan_width": 900.0,
                        "elevation_bottom_y": 1000.0,
                        "elevation_top_y": 3100.0,
                        "elevation_height": 2100.0,
                    },
                    {
                        "kind": "window",
                        "plan_index": 1,
                        "elevation_index": 10,
                        "elevation_bottom_y": 1900.0,
                        "elevation_top_y": 3400.0,
                        "elevation_height": 1500.0,
                    },
                ],
            },
            {
                "facade_number": 2,
                "plan_side": "left",
                "opening_pairs": [
                    {
                        "kind": "door",
                        "plan_index": 0,
                        "plan_width": 900.0,
                        "elevation_bottom_y": 5000.0,
                        "elevation_top_y": 7100.0,
                        "elevation_height": 2100.0,
                    },
                    {
                        "kind": "window",
                        "plan_index": 1,
                        "elevation_index": 20,
                        "elevation_bottom_y": 5900.0,
                        "elevation_top_y": 7400.0,
                        "elevation_height": 1500.0,
                    },
                ],
            },
        ],
    }

    result = (
        calculate_window_vertical_measurements(
            analysis
        )
    )

    assert result["ok"], result
    assert result["window_count"] == 2, result

    for window in result["windows"]:
        assert abs(
            window["window_bottom_z_cm"]
            - 90.0
        ) < 1.0e-6, window

        assert abs(
            window["window_height_cm"]
            - 150.0
        ) < 1.0e-6, window

        assert abs(
            window["window_top_z_cm"]
            - 240.0
        ) < 1.0e-6, window

    assert (
        result[
            "interfloor_thickness_cm"
        ]
        == 35.0
    )

    assert (
        result[
            "floor_to_floor_cm"
        ]
        == 335.0
    )

    print(
        "WINDOW VERTICAL MEASUREMENTS V1 SELF-TEST: OK"
    )

    print(
        "SILL   : 90.00 cm"
    )

    print(
        "HEIGHT : 150.00 cm"
    )

    print(
        "TOP    : 240.00 cm"
    )

    print(
        "SLAB   : 35.00 cm"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    self_test()

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path


ENGINE = "CAD3D_MULTI_FLOOR_3D_PREPARE_V1"


def _project_root():
    return (
        Path(__file__)
        .resolve()
        .parents[2]
    )


def _assignment_signature(
    assignments,
):
    rows = []

    for row in assignments or []:
        rows.append(
            (
                int(
                    row["selection_index"]
                ),
                str(
                    row["floor_name"]
                ),
                int(
                    row["floor_order"]
                ),
                round(
                    float(
                        row["floor_height_cm"]
                    ),
                    6,
                ),
                round(
                    float(
                        row[
                            "interfloor_height_cm"
                        ]
                    ),
                    6,
                ),
                tuple(
                    round(
                        float(v),
                        6,
                    )
                    for v in row[
                        "source_bounds"
                    ]
                ),
            )
        )

    return tuple(
        sorted(rows)
    )


def validate_floor_assignments(
    window,
):
    floors = list(
        getattr(
            window,
            "confirmed_floor_plans",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    assignments = list(
        getattr(
            window,
            "floor_plan_assignments",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    expected = {
        int(
            row["selection_index"]
        )
        for row in floors
    }

    actual = {
        int(
            row["selection_index"]
        )
        for row in assignments
    }

    names = [
        str(
            row["floor_name"]
        )
        for row in assignments
    ]

    duplicate_names = (
        len(names)
        != len(set(names))
    )

    complete = bool(
        expected
        and expected == actual
    )

    return {
        "ok":
            bool(
                complete
                and not duplicate_names
            ),

        "complete":
            complete,

        "duplicate_names":
            duplicate_names,

        "floor_count":
            len(expected),

        "assignment_count":
            len(actual),

        "signature":
            _assignment_signature(
                assignments
            ),
    }



def build_canonical_structural_wall_package(
    geometry,
    cad_meta=None,
):
    """
    Canonical structural WALL package.

    Wall recognition authority is the dedicated wall detector:
    opposing wall faces + thickness + overlap + network topology.

    Layer names and layer grouping are provenance/compatibility metadata only.
    """

    from src.cad.wall_detector import detect_walls

    geometry = [
        item
        for item in list(geometry or [])
        if isinstance(item, dict)
    ]

    if not geometry:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Canonical wall package: CAD geometry is empty."
        )

    wall_result = detect_walls(
        geometry,
        cad_meta=cad_meta,
    )

    closed_wall_paths = list(
        wall_result.get(
            "closed_wall_paths",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    if not closed_wall_paths:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Canonical wall package: "
            "wall object recognition produced no closed wall mass."
        )

    wall_source = list(
        wall_result.get(
            "wall_source_geometry",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    primary_wall_layer = str(
        wall_result.get(
            "compatibility_primary_layer",
            "",
        )
        or ""
    ).strip()

    contributing_layers = [
        str(value or "").strip()
        for value in list(
            wall_result.get(
                "contributing_layers_provenance",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )
        if str(value or "").strip()
    ]

    # Compatibility only: existing window/door call sites still expect a
    # real source-layer identifier. This value is derived AFTER walls were
    # recognized and has zero authority over the wall decision itself.
    if not primary_wall_layer and contributing_layers:
        primary_wall_layer = contributing_layers[0]

    profile = {
        "engine": "CAD3D_WALL_OBJECT_RECOGNITION",
        "wall_detection_authority":
            "ENTITY_SEGMENT_GEOMETRY_TOPOLOGY",
        "layer_name_decision_weight": 0.0,
        "layer_group_decision_weight": 0.0,
        "primary_structural_layer":
            primary_wall_layer or None,
        "structural_layers":
            list(contributing_layers),
        "opening_layers": [],
        "gap_families": [
            {
                "gap": float(value),
                "source": "recognized_wall_thickness",
            }
            for value in list(
                wall_result.get(
                    "selected_family_gaps_mm",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            )
        ],
        "confidence": 1.0,
        "layer_scores": [],
    }

    cleanup_report = {
        "engine": "CAD3D_WALL_OBJECT_RECOGNITION",
        "source_segment_count": int(
            wall_result.get("source_segment_count", 0)
            or 0
        ),
        "candidate_pair_count": int(
            wall_result.get("candidate_pair_count", 0)
            or 0
        ),
        "selected_pair_count": int(
            wall_result.get("selected_pair_count", 0)
            or 0
        ),
        "closed_wall_count": len(closed_wall_paths),
        "layer_name_decision_weight": 0.0,
        "layer_group_decision_weight": 0.0,
        "family_audit": list(
            wall_result.get("family_audit", [])
            or []
        ),
    }

    print(
        "CANONICAL WALL OBJECTS",
        "| PRIMARY COMPATIBILITY LAYER:",
        primary_wall_layer or None,
        "| PROVENANCE LAYERS:",
        contributing_layers,
        "| CLOSED:",
        len(closed_wall_paths),
        "| LAYER-NAME WEIGHT: 0",
        "| LAYER-GROUP WEIGHT: 0",
    )

    return {
        "profile": profile,
        "primary_structural_layer":
            primary_wall_layer or None,
        "structural_layers":
            list(contributing_layers),
        "active_wall_layers":
            list(contributing_layers),
        "wall_source_geometry":
            list(wall_source),
        "cleaned_wall_paths":
            list(closed_wall_paths),
        "cleanup_report":
            cleanup_report,
        "closed_wall_count":
            len(closed_wall_paths),
        "rejected_nonwall_layers": [],
        "wall_detection":
            dict(wall_result),
    }

def prepare_multi_floor_3d(
    window,
):
    from src.cad.cad_intelligence import (
        infer_cad_profile,
    )

    from src.cad.door_detector import (
        detect_doors,
    )

    from src.cad.window_detector import (
        detect_windows,
    )

    from src.cad.generic_cad_cleanup import (
        build_clean_visible_polylines,
    )

    from src.cad.floor_geometry import (
        prepare_floor_geometry,
    )

    validation = (
        validate_floor_assignments(
            window
        )
    )

    if not validation["ok"]:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "T\u00fcm kat planlar\u0131 benzersiz kat isimleriyle "
            "tan\u0131mlanmadan haz\u0131rl\u0131k yap\u0131lamaz."
        )

    assignments = sorted(
        list(
            window.floor_plan_assignments
        ),
        key=lambda row: (
            int(
                row["floor_order"]
            ),
            int(
                row["selection_index"]
            ),
        ),
    )

    cad_meta = (
        getattr(
            window,
            "cad_data",
            None,
        )
        or getattr(
            window.preview,
            "cad_data",
            None,
        )
    )

    packages = []
    audit_rows = []

    for assignment in assignments:
        bounds = tuple(
            float(value)
            for value
            in assignment[
                "source_bounds"
            ]
        )

        summary = (
            window.preview.cad_summary_for_bounds(
                bounds
            )
        )

        if not isinstance(
            summary,
            dict,
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                f'{assignment["floor_name"]}: CAD summary olu\u015fturulamad\u0131.'
            )

        geometry = list(
            summary.get(
                "geometry",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        if not geometry:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f'{assignment["floor_name"]}: CAD geometrisi bo\u015f.'
            )

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # EXISTING STRUCTURAL LAYER RULE
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # CAD3D_CANONICAL_STRUCTURAL_WALL_PACKAGE_V2
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        wall_package = (
            build_canonical_structural_wall_package(
                geometry,
                cad_meta=cad_meta,
            )
        )

        profile = wall_package[
            "profile"
        ]

        target_layer = str(
            wall_package[
                "primary_structural_layer"
            ]
            or ""
        ).strip()

        structural_layers = list(
            wall_package[
                "structural_layers"
            ]
            or []
        )

        active_wall_layers = list(
            wall_package[
                "active_wall_layers"
            ]
            or []
        )

        wall_source = list(
            wall_package[
                "wall_source_geometry"
            ]
            or []
        )

        closed_wall_paths = list(
            wall_package[
                "cleaned_wall_paths"
            ]
            or []
        )

        cleanup_report = dict(
            wall_package[
                "cleanup_report"
            ]
            or {}
        )

        print(
            str(
                assignment[
                    "floor_name"
                ]
            ),
            "| WALL LAYERS:",
            active_wall_layers,
            "| CLOSED:",
            len(
                closed_wall_paths
            ),
        )

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # EXISTING DOOR DETECTOR - UNCHANGED
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        door_result = detect_doors(
            geometry
        )

        all_doors = list(
            door_result.get(
                "doors",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        bridge_doors = [
            door
            for door in all_doors
            if bool(
                door.get(
                    "bridge_ready",
                    False,
                )
            )
        ]

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # EXISTING WINDOW DETECTOR - UNCHANGED
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        window_result = detect_windows(
            geometry,
            structural_layer=target_layer,
        )

        windows = list(
            window_result.get(
                "windows",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        rooflights = list(
            window_result.get(
                "rooflights",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        # CAD3D_EXACT_ADJACENT_STAIR_FLOOR_RULE_V35
        #
        # Stair voids are topology-driven, never user-selected:
        # FLOOR(N) gets a stair void iff immediate lower storey N-1 exists.
        #
        _floor_order = int(
            assignment["floor_order"]
        )

        _existing_floor_orders = {
            int(row["floor_order"])
            for row in assignments
            if isinstance(row, dict)
            and "floor_order" in row
        }

        _apply_stair_voids = bool(
            (_floor_order - 1)
            in _existing_floor_orders
        )

        floor_geometry = prepare_floor_geometry(
            geometry=geometry,
            closed_wall_paths=closed_wall_paths,
            target_layer=target_layer,
            source_to_mm=float(
                cleanup_report.get(
                    "source_to_mm",
                    1.0,
                )
                or 1.0
            ),
            floor_order=_floor_order,
            floor_name=str(
                assignment["floor_name"]
            ),
            apply_stair_voids=_apply_stair_voids,
            opening_records=(
                list(all_doors)
                + list(windows)
            ),
        )

        if not bool(
            floor_geometry.get(
                "ok",
                False,
            )
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                str(
                    assignment["floor_name"]
                )
                + ": zemin dış konturu hesaplanamadı. "
                + str(
                    floor_geometry.get(
                        "reason",
                        "unknown",
                    )
                )
            )

        # CAD3D_MAIN_ENTRANCE_HEIGHT_TO_GROUND_FLOOR_V1
        resolved_floor_height_cm = float(
            assignment["floor_height_cm"]
        )
        _cad3d_auto_height_result = getattr(
            window,
            "current_main_entrance_door_result",
            None,
        )
        _cad3d_facade_result = getattr(
            window,
            "current_facade_match_result",
            None,
        )
        if (
            int(assignment["floor_order"]) == 0
            and isinstance(_cad3d_auto_height_result, dict)
            and bool(_cad3d_auto_height_result.get("ok", False))
            and isinstance(_cad3d_facade_result, dict)
        ):
            try:
                _cad3d_auto_height_cm = float(
                    _cad3d_auto_height_result["ground_floor_height_cm"]
                )
                _cad3d_result_height_cm = float(
                    _cad3d_facade_result["detected_ground_floor_height_cm"]
                )
                _cad3d_auto_token = str(
                    _cad3d_auto_height_result.get("analysis_token") or ""
                )
                _cad3d_result_token = str(
                    _cad3d_facade_result.get("main_entrance_analysis_token") or ""
                )
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
                _cad3d_auto_height_cm = None
                _cad3d_result_height_cm = None
                _cad3d_auto_token = ""
                _cad3d_result_token = ""
            if (
                _cad3d_auto_height_cm is not None
                and _cad3d_result_height_cm is not None
                and 100.0 <= _cad3d_auto_height_cm <= 600.0
                and abs(_cad3d_auto_height_cm - _cad3d_result_height_cm) <= 0.01
                and _cad3d_auto_token
                and _cad3d_auto_token == _cad3d_result_token
            ):
                resolved_floor_height_cm = _cad3d_auto_height_cm
                print(
                    "GROUND FLOOR HEIGHT SOURCE: MAIN ENTRANCE DOOR |",
                    round(resolved_floor_height_cm, 2),
                    "cm",
                )

        package = {
            "selection_index":
                int(
                    assignment[
                        "selection_index"
                    ]
                ),

            "floor_name":
                str(
                    assignment[
                        "floor_name"
                    ]
                ),

            "floor_order":
                int(
                    assignment[
                        "floor_order"
                    ]
                ),

            "floor_height_cm":
                resolved_floor_height_cm,

            "interfloor_height_cm":
                float(
                    assignment[
                        "interfloor_height_cm"
                    ]
                ),

            "source_bounds":
                bounds,

            # This is the exact crop state the existing
            # 3D procedure will receive later.
            "cad_crop_summary":
                summary,

            "geometry":
                geometry,

            "cad_profile":
                profile,

            "target_layer":
                target_layer,

            "structural_layers":
                structural_layers,

            "active_wall_layers":
                active_wall_layers,

            "wall_source_geometry":
                wall_source,

            "cleaned_wall_paths":
                closed_wall_paths,

            "cleanup_report":
                cleanup_report,

            # CAD3D_FLOOR_GEOMETRY_PACKAGE_V1
            "floor_geometry":
                floor_geometry,

            "door_result":
                door_result,

            "doors":
                all_doors,

            "bridge_doors":
                bridge_doors,

            "window_result":
                window_result,

            "windows":
                windows,

            "rooflights":
                rooflights,

            # Existing floor metadata shape for the
            # current 3D procedure.
            "approved_floor_info": {
                "kind":
                    "floor",

                "index":
                    int(
                        assignment[
                            "floor_order"
                        ]
                    ),

                "label":
                    str(
                        assignment[
                            "floor_name"
                        ]
                    ),
            },
        }

        packages.append(
            package
        )

        audit_rows.append(
            {
                "floor_name":
                    package[
                        "floor_name"
                    ],

                "floor_order":
                    package[
                        "floor_order"
                    ],

                "target_layer":
                    target_layer,

                "geometry_count":
                    len(
                        geometry
                    ),

                "closed_wall_paths":
                    len(
                        closed_wall_paths
                    ),

                "detected_doors":
                    len(
                        all_doors
                    ),

                "bridge_ready_doors":
                    len(
                        bridge_doors
                    ),

                "windows":
                    len(
                        windows
                    ),

                "rooflights":
                    len(
                        rooflights
                    ),

                "floor_height_cm":
                    package[
                        "floor_height_cm"
                    ],

                "interfloor_height_cm":
                    package[
                        "interfloor_height_cm"
                    ],
            }
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # CAD3D_FLOOR_GEOMETRY_AUDIT_V1
    floor_geometry_log_path = (
        _project_root()
        / "logs"
        / "floor_geometry_prepare.json"
    )

    floor_geometry_log_path.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    floor_geometry_log_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "engine":
                    "CAD3D_FLOOR_GEOMETRY_PREPARE_V1",

                "floor_count":
                    len(
                        packages
                    ),

                "floors":
                    [
                        {
                            "floor_name":
                                package[
                                    "floor_name"
                                ],

                            "floor_order":
                                package[
                                    "floor_order"
                                ],

                            "floor_geometry":
                                package[
                                    "floor_geometry"
                                ],
                        }
                        for package
                        in packages
                    ],
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print("")
    print(
        "=== FLOOR GEOMETRY PREPARE V1 ==="
    )

    for package in packages:
        _floor_geo = package[
            "floor_geometry"
        ]

        print(
            package[
                "floor_name"
            ],
            "| OUTER:",
            len(
                _floor_geo.get(
                    "outer_boundary_source",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            ),
            "| STAIR CANDIDATES:",
            len(
                _floor_geo.get(
                    "stair_void_candidates",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            ),
            "| STAIR APPLIED:",
            len(
                _floor_geo.get(
                    "applied_stair_voids",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            ),
            "| APPLY:",
            bool(
                _floor_geo.get(
                    "apply_stair_voids",
                    False,
                )
            ),
        )

    print(
        "LOG:",
        str(
            floor_geometry_log_path
        ),
    )

    print(
        "=== END FLOOR GEOMETRY PREPARE V1 ==="
    )
    print("")

    # MEMORY STATE FOR NEXT STEP:
    # sequential Max sending.
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    window.multi_floor_3d_packages = (
        packages
    )

    window.multi_floor_3d_prepared = True


    window._cad3d_prepared_assignment_signature = (
        validation[
            "signature"
        ]
    )

    window.floor_plan_send_order = list(
        assignments
    )

    payload = {
        "engine":
            ENGINE,

        "ok":
            True,

        "floor_count":
            len(
                packages
            ),

        "send_order":
            [
                row[
                    "floor_name"
                ]
                for row in packages
            ],

        "floors":
            audit_rows,
    }

    log_path = (
        _project_root()
        / "logs"
        / "multi_floor_3d_prepare.json"
    )

    log_path.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    log_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            payload,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print("")
    print(
        "=== MULTI FLOOR 3D PREPARE ==="
    )

    for row in audit_rows:
        print(
            row["floor_name"],
            "| WALLS:",
            row["closed_wall_paths"],
            "| DOORS:",
            row["detected_doors"],
            "| BRIDGE:",
            row["bridge_ready_doors"],
            "| WINDOWS:",
            row["windows"],
            "| ROOFLIGHTS:",
            row["rooflights"],
        )

    print(
        "SEND ORDER:",
        " -> ".join(
            payload[
                "send_order"
            ]
        ),
    )

    print(
        "READY FOR EXISTING 3D PROCEDURE: True"
    )

    print(
        "LOG:",
        str(
            log_path
        ),
    )

    print(
        "=== END MULTI FLOOR 3D PREPARE ==="
    )

    print("")

    return payload


def self_test():
    class Dummy:
        confirmed_floor_plans = [
            {
                "selection_index": 1,
            },
            {
                "selection_index": 2,
            },
        ]

        floor_plan_assignments = [
            {
                "selection_index": 2,
                "floor_name": "1. Kat",
                "floor_order": 1,
                "floor_height_cm": 280.0,
                "interfloor_height_cm": 35.0,
                "source_bounds": [
                    10,
                    10,
                    20,
                    20,
                ],
            },
            {
                "selection_index": 1,
                "floor_name": "Giri\u015f Kat",
                "floor_order": 0,
                "floor_height_cm": 280.0,
                "interfloor_height_cm": 0.0,
                "source_bounds": [
                    0,
                    0,
                    10,
                    10,
                ],
            },
        ]

    result = validate_floor_assignments(
        Dummy()
    )

    assert result["ok"]
    assert result["floor_count"] == 2

    print(
        "MULTI FLOOR 3D PREPARE V1 SELF-TEST: OK"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    self_test()

--- _write_window_top_edge_records_v1 | 2725-3266 ---
def _write_window_top_edge_records_v1(
    window,
    package,
    runtime,
):
    import json as _window_top_json
    import math as _window_top_math

    windows = list(
        getattr(
            window,
            "current_window_detections",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    pivot = runtime.get(
        "pivot",
        {},
    )

    floor_order = int(
        package.get(
            "floor_order",
            0,
        )
    )

    facade_path = (
        _project_root()
        / "logs"
        / "facade_windows_button_RESULT.json"
    )

    if not facade_path.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Facade window result not found: "
            + str(facade_path)
        )

    facade_data = _window_top_json.loads(
        facade_path.read_text(
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
    )

    facade_windows = list(
        facade_data.get(
            "plan_guided_facade_windows",
            [],
        )
        or []
    )

    facade_by_index = {}

    for row in facade_windows:
        if not isinstance(row, dict):
            continue

        if str(
            row.get(
                "kind",
                "",
            )
            or ""
        ).strip().lower() != "window":
            continue

        try:
            row_floor = int(
                row.get(
                    "floor_order"
                )
            )

            source_index = int(
                row.get(
                    "source_window_index"
                )
            )

        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue

        if row_floor != floor_order:
            continue

        if source_index < 0:
            continue

        if row.get(
            "height_eligible",
            True,
        ) is False:
            continue

        try:
            elevation_height = float(
                row.get(
                    "elevation_height"
                )
            )

        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue

        if elevation_height <= 0.0:
            continue

        previous = facade_by_index.get(
            source_index
        )

        if previous is None:
            facade_by_index[
                source_index
            ] = row

        else:
            try:
                previous_cost = float(
                    previous.get(
                        "selection_cost",
                        1.0e30,
                    )
                )
            except Exception:
                previous_cost = 1.0e30

            try:
                new_cost = float(
                    row.get(
                        "selection_cost",
                        1.0e30,
                    )
                )
            except Exception:
                new_cost = 1.0e30

            if new_cost < previous_cost:
                facade_by_index[
                    source_index
                ] = row

    json_target = (
        _project_root()
        / "data"
        / "cache"
        / "max_bridge"
        / "window_top_edge_records.json"
    )

    txt_target = (
        _project_root()
        / "data"
        / "cache"
        / "max_bridge"
        / "window_top_edge_records.txt"
    )

    json_target.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    records = []
    missing_jamb = []
    no_facade_height = []

    source_to_mm = 1.0

    for source_index, item in enumerate(
        windows
    ):
        display_index = source_index + 1

        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            missing_jamb.append(
                display_index
            )
            continue

        jamb_a = item.get(
            "jamb_a"
        )

        jamb_b = item.get(
            "jamb_b"
        )

        if (
            not isinstance(
                jamb_a,
                (list, tuple),
            )
            or len(jamb_a) < 2
            or not isinstance(
                jamb_b,
                (list, tuple),
            )
            or len(jamb_b) < 2
        ):
            missing_jamb.append(
                display_index
            )
            continue

        try:
            ax = float(jamb_a[0])
            ay = float(jamb_a[1])
            bx = float(jamb_b[0])
            by = float(jamb_b[1])

        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            missing_jamb.append(
                display_index
            )
            continue

        facade_row = facade_by_index.get(
            source_index
        )

        if facade_row is None:
            no_facade_height.append(
                display_index
            )

            # CAD3D_NON_FACADE_WINDOW_HEIGHT_FALLBACK_V1
            #
            # Valid plan windows without a facade elevation
            # match must still enter the existing Max window
            # topology pipeline. Their real detected jambs are
            # preserved; only vertical height comes from the
            # existing Window Height UI value.
            try:
                raw_height_cm = float(
                    window.window_height.value()
                )

            except Exception as exc:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Non-facade window height fallback unavailable "
                    + f"(W{display_index:03d}): {exc}"
                )

            if (
                not _window_top_math.isfinite(
                    raw_height_cm
                )
                or raw_height_cm <= 0.0
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Non-facade window height is invalid "
                    + f"(W{display_index:03d}): {raw_height_cm}"
                )

            height_cm = (
                _window_top_math.floor(
                    raw_height_cm * 2.0 + 0.5
                )
                / 2.0
            )

            source_window_id = (
                "W"
                + str(
                    display_index
                ).zfill(3)
            )

            records.append(
                {
                    "window_id":
                        source_window_id,

                    "source_window_index":
                        source_index,

                    "jamb_a": [
                        ax,
                        ay,
                    ],

                    "jamb_b": [
                        bx,
                        by,
                    ],

                    "facade_number":
                        None,

                    "elevation_height_raw":
                        None,

                    "window_height_raw_cm":
                        raw_height_cm,

                    "window_height_cm":
                        height_cm,

                    "height_source":
                        "UI_WINDOW_HEIGHT_NON_FACADE",
                }
            )

            continue

        raw_height_cm = (
            float(
                facade_row[
                    "elevation_height"
                ]
            )
            * source_to_mm
            / 10.0
        )

        height_cm = (
            _window_top_math.floor(
                raw_height_cm * 2.0 + 0.5
            )
            / 2.0
        )

        source_window_id = str(
            facade_row.get(
                "source_window_id",
                "",
            )
            or ""
        ).strip()

        if not source_window_id:
            source_window_id = (
                "W"
                + str(
                    display_index
                ).zfill(3)
            )

        records.append(
            {
                "window_id":
                    source_window_id,

                "source_window_index":
                    source_index,

                "jamb_a": [
                    ax,
                    ay,
                ],

                "jamb_b": [
                    bx,
                    by,
                ],

                "elevation_height_raw":

                        facade_row[
                            "elevation_height"
                        ]
                    ),

                "window_height_raw_cm":
                    raw_height_cm,

                "window_height_cm":
                    height_cm,

                "height_source":
                    "FACADE_ELEVATION_HEIGHT",
            }
        )

    payload = {
        "version": 2,

        "purpose":
            "WINDOW_TOP_EDGE_MOVE",

        "floor_name":
            str(
                package.get(
                    "floor_name",
                    "",
                )
            ),

        "floor_order":
            floor_order,

        "pivot_x_mm":
            float(
                pivot.get(
                    "pivot_x",
                    0.0,
                )
            ),

        "pivot_y_mm":
            float(
                pivot.get(
                    "pivot_y",
                    0.0,
                )
            ),

        "source_window_count":
            len(windows),

        "mapped_height_count":
            len(records),

        "missing_jamb_indices":
            missing_jamb,

        "no_facade_height_indices":
            no_facade_height,

        "source_to_mm":
            source_to_mm,

        "windows":
            records,
    }

    json_target.write_text(
        _window_top_json.dumps(
            payload,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    txt_lines = [
        "# CAD3D_WINDOW_TOP_EDGE_RECORDS_V2",
        "# window_id|ax_mm|ay_mm|bx_mm|by_mm|height_cm",
    ]

    for row in records:
        txt_lines.append(
            "|".join(
                [
                    str(
                        row[
                            "window_id"
                        ]
                    ),
                    repr(
                        float(
                            row[
                                "jamb_a"
                            ][0]
                        )
                    ),
                    repr(
                        float(
                            row[
                                "jamb_a"
                            ][1]
                        )
                    ),
                    repr(
                        float(
                            row[
                                "jamb_b"
                            ][0]
                        )
                    ),
                    repr(
                        float(
                            row[
                                "jamb_b"
                            ][1]
                        )
                    ),
                    repr(
                        float(
                            row[
                                "window_height_cm"
                            ]
                        )
                    ),
                ]
            )
        )

    txt_target.write_text(
        "\n".join(
            txt_lines
        )
        + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print("")
    print(
        "=== WINDOW TOP EDGE RECORDS V2 ==="
    )
    print(
        "FLOOR:",
        payload[
            "floor_name"
        ],
    )
    print(
        "SOURCE WINDOWS:",
        len(windows),
    )
    print(
        "FACADE HEIGHTS:",
        len(records),
    )
    print(
        "NO FACADE HEIGHT:",
        no_facade_height,
    )
    print(
        "MISSING JAMB:",
        missing_jamb,
    )
    print(
        "TXT:",
        str(txt_target),
    )
    print(
        "=== END WINDOW TOP EDGE RECORDS V2 ==="
    )
    print("")

    return txt_target

--- _cad3d_compute_floor_base_z_cm_v4 | 3874-3985 ---
def _cad3d_compute_floor_base_z_cm_v4(
    window,
    package,
):
    """
    Compute BASE Z from the already-approved ordered multi-floor packages.

    This runs BEFORE _on_create_3d_clicked(), so it intentionally does not
    read cad_3d_options.txt. The current floor's interfloor gap is applied
    before its base, matching the proven floor stack:
      Ground: 0
      1. Kat: ground_height + 1.Kat.interfloor_gap
    """

    import math as _base_math

    target_order = int(
        package["floor_order"]
    )

    packages = sorted(
        list(
            getattr(
                window,
                "multi_floor_3d_packages",
                [],
            )
            or []
        ),
        key=lambda row: int(
            row.get(
                "floor_order",
                -999,
            )
        ),
    )

    if not packages:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Room floor V4: multi-floor packages missing."
        )

    orders = [
        int(row.get("floor_order", -999))
        for row in packages
    ]

    expected = list(
        range(len(packages))
    )

    if orders != expected:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Room floor V4: floor order is not contiguous."
        )

    base_z_cm = 0.0

    for row in packages:
        order = int(
            row["floor_order"]
        )

        if order > target_order:
            break

        if order > 0:
            gap_cm = float(
                row.get(
                    "interfloor_height_cm",
                    0.0,
                )
                or 0.0
            )

            if (
                not _base_math.isfinite(gap_cm)
                or gap_cm < 0.0
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Room floor V4: invalid interfloor gap."
                )

            base_z_cm += gap_cm

        if order == target_order:
            if not _base_math.isfinite(
                base_z_cm
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Room floor V4: non-finite BASE Z."
                )

            return base_z_cm

        height_cm = float(
            row["floor_height_cm"]
        )

        if (
            not _base_math.isfinite(height_cm)
            or height_cm <= 0.0
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Room floor V4: invalid previous floor height."
            )

        base_z_cm += height_cm

    raise RuntimeError(
        "Room floor V4: target floor was not found."
    )

==============================================================================================================

--- _cad3d_collect_facade_window_boxes_legacy | 62-303 ---
    def _cad3d_collect_facade_window_boxes_legacy(
        self,
    ):
        """
        CAD3D_ALL_FACADE_WINDOW_SOURCE_V1

        Visualization-only resolver.

        Uses every detected elevation facade and the existing
        elevation opening detector.

        Does NOT use plan opening_pairs.
        Does NOT modify floor selection.
        Does NOT modify Max metadata.
        """

        result = getattr(
            self,
            "current_facade_match_result",
            None,
        )

        if not isinstance(
            result,
            dict,
        ):
            return []

        facades = list(
            result.get(
                "elevation_facades",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        if not facades:
            return []

        from src.cad.structural_window_graph import (
            detect_graph_facade_windows,
        )

        boxes = []
        records = []
        seen = set()

        for facade in facades:

            facade_number = facade.get(
                "facade_number"
            )

            bbox = facade.get(
                "bbox"
            )

            if (
                not isinstance(
                    bbox,
                    (list, tuple),
                )
                or len(bbox) != 4
            ):
                continue

            try:
                facade_bounds = tuple(
                    float(value)
                    for value in bbox
                )
            except Exception:
                continue

            try:
                summary = (
                    self.preview.cad_summary_for_bounds(
                        facade_bounds
                    )
                )

                geometry = list(
                    summary.get(
                        "geometry",
                        [],
                    )
                    or []
                )

            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "FACADE WINDOW REGION READ FAILED:",
                    facade_number,
                    repr(exc),
                )

                continue

            if not geometry:
                continue

            try:
                detection = (
                    detect_graph_facade_windows(
                        geometry,
                        facade_bounds,
                    )
                )

            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "FACADE WINDOW DETECTOR FAILED:",
                    facade_number,
                    repr(exc),
                )

                continue

            entities = list(
                detection.get(
                    "entities",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            )

            facade_window_count = 0

            for entity in entities:

                if (
                    str(
                        entity.get(
                            "kind",
                            "",
                        )
                        or ""
                    )
                    .strip()
                    .lower()
                    != "window"
                ):
                    continue

                window_bbox = entity.get(
                    "bbox"
                )

                if (
                    not isinstance(
                        window_bbox,
                        (list, tuple),
                    )
                    or len(window_bbox) != 4
                ):
                    continue

                try:
                    clean = tuple(
                        float(value)
                        for value in window_bbox
                    )
                except Exception:
                    continue

                # Stable de-duplication only.
                # Nested-frame resolution is already performed
                # by elevation_opening_detector.
                key = tuple(
                    round(
                        value,
                        3,
                    )
                    for value in clean
                )

                if key in seen:
                    continue

                seen.add(
                    key
                )

                boxes.append(
                    clean
                )

                facade_window_count += 1

                records.append(
                    {
                        "facade_number":
                            facade_number,

                        "region_id":
                            facade.get(
                                "region_id"
                            ),

                        "entity_id":
                            entity.get(
                                "entity_id"
                            ),

                        "bbox":
                            clean,

                        "bottom_y":
                            entity.get(
                                "drawing_bottom_y",
                                entity.get(
                                    "bottom_y"
                                ),
                            ),

                        "top_y":
                            entity.get(
                                "drawing_top_y",
                                entity.get(
                                    "top_y"
                                ),
                            ),
                    }
                )

            print(
                "FACADE",
                facade_number,
                "| WINDOWS:",
                facade_window_count,
            )

        self._cad3d_facade_window_records = (
            records
        )

        print(
            "ALL FACADE WINDOWS:",
            len(boxes),
        )

        return boxes

--- _cad3d_collect_facade_window_boxes | 306-395 ---
    def _cad3d_collect_facade_window_boxes(
        self,
    ):
        """
        CAD3D_PLAN_GUIDED_FACADE_OVERLAY_V2

        When plan-guided validation exists:
            return ONLY plan-approved physical facade windows.

        IMPORTANT:
        Do NOT run the experimental graph detector again.

        If plan-guided data does not exist yet:
            preserve the previous collector behavior.
        """

        analysis = getattr(
            self,
            "current_facade_match_result",
            None,
        )

        if (
            isinstance(
                analysis,
                dict,
            )
            and bool(
                analysis.get(
                    "plan_guided_applied",
                    False,
                )
            )
        ):

            accepted = list(
                analysis.get(
                    "plan_guided_facade_windows",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            )

            boxes = []

            for row in accepted:

                try:
                    x0, y0, x1, y1 = (
                        float(v)
                        for v in row[
                            "elevation_bbox"
                        ]
                    )

                except Exception:
                    continue

                if (
                    x1 <= x0
                    or y1 <= y0
                ):
                    continue

                boxes.append(
                    (
                        x0,
                        y0,
                        x1,
                        y1,
                    )
                )

            print(
                "PLAN GUIDED OVERLAY"
                " | accepted:",
                len(
                    boxes
                ),
            )

            return boxes


        # Before plan-guided analysis exists, preserve the
        # currently installed behavior.
        return (
            self
            ._cad3d_collect_facade_window_boxes_legacy()
        )

                pass

--- _cad3d_show_facade_window_overlay | 416-846 ---
    def _cad3d_show_facade_window_overlay(
        self,
    ) -> int:
        """
        CAD3D_FACADE_WINDOW_OVERLAY_SYNC_V2

        Viewport-only visualization.

        - detector data is unchanged
        - selection system is unchanged
        - overlay follows viewport resize
        - overlay follows scroll/pan
        - overlay follows canvas resize/zoom
        - overlay never consumes mouse events
        """

        from PySide6.QtCore import (
            QEvent,
            QPoint,
            QRect,
            Qt,
        )

        from PySide6.QtGui import (
            QColor,
            QPainter,
            QPen,
        )

        from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
            QWidget,
        )

        boxes = (
            self._cad3d_collect_facade_window_boxes()
        )

        if not boxes:
            self._cad3d_hide_facade_window_overlay()

            self.status.setText(
                "Cephede e\u015fle\u015fmi\u015f pencere bulunamad\u0131."
            )

            return 0

        preview = self.preview
        canvas = preview.canvas
        viewport = preview.viewport()


        class _FacadeWindowOverlay(
            QWidget
        ):
            def __init__(
                overlay_self,
                owner,
            ):
                QWidget.__init__(
                    overlay_self,
                    owner.preview.viewport(),
                )

                overlay_self.owner = owner

                overlay_self.setAttribute(
                    Qt.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents,
                    True,
                )

                overlay_self.setAttribute(
                    Qt.WA_NoSystemBackground,
                    True,
                )

                overlay_self.setAttribute(
                    Qt.WA_TranslucentBackground,
                    True,
                )


            def sync_to_viewport(
                overlay_self,
                *_args,
            ):
                try:
                    viewport_local = (
                        overlay_self.owner
                        .preview
                        .viewport()
                    )

                    rect = (
                        viewport_local.rect()
                    )

                    if (
                        overlay_self.geometry()
                        != rect
                    ):
                        overlay_self.setGeometry(
                            rect
                        )

                    overlay_self.raise_()
                    overlay_self.update()

                except Exception:
                    pass


            def eventFilter(
                overlay_self,
                obj,
                event,
            ):
                event_type = (
                    event.type()
                )

                if event_type in (
                    QEvent.Resize,
                    QEvent.Move,
                    QEvent.Show,
                    QEvent.Wheel,
                ):
                    overlay_self.sync_to_viewport()

                # CRITICAL:
                # Never consume viewport/canvas events.
                return False


            def paintEvent(
                overlay_self,
                event,
            ):
                owner = (
                    overlay_self.owner
                )

                boxes_local = list(
                    getattr(
                        owner,
                        "_cad3d_facade_window_boxes",
                        [],
                    )
                    or []
                )

                if not boxes_local:
                    return

                preview_local = (
                    owner.preview
                )

                canvas_local = (
                    preview_local.canvas
                )

                viewport_local = (
                    preview_local.viewport()
                )

                painter = QPainter(
                    overlay_self
                )

                painter.setRenderHint(
                    QPainter.Antialiasing,
                    False,
                )

                pen = QPen(
                    QColor(
                        255,
                        145,
                        0,
                    )
                )

                pen.setWidth(
                    3
                )

                pen.setCosmetic(
                    True
                )

                painter.setPen(
                    pen
                )

                painter.setBrush(
                    QColor(
                        255,
                        145,
                        0,
                        55,
                    )
                )


                def cad_to_view(
                    x,
                    y,
                ):
                    result = None

                    try:
                        result = (
                            canvas_local
                            .cad_to_canvas_point(
                                float(x),
                                float(y),
                            )
                        )

                    except Exception:
                        try:
                            result = (
                                canvas_local
                                .cad_to_canvas_point(
                                    (
                                        float(x),
                                        float(y),
                                    )
                                )
                            )

                        except Exception:
                            return None

                    try:
                        canvas_point = QPoint(
                            int(
                                round(
                                    float(
                                        result.x()
                                    )
                                )
                            ),
                            int(
                                round(
                                    float(
                                        result.y()
                                    )
                                )
                            ),
                        )

                    except Exception:
                        try:
                            canvas_point = QPoint(
                                int(
                                    round(
                                        float(
                                            result[0]
                                        )
                                    )
                                ),
                                int(
                                    round(
                                        float(
                                            result[1]
                                        )
                                    )
                                ),
                            )

                        except Exception:
                            return None

                    return (
                        canvas_local.mapTo(
                            viewport_local,
                            canvas_point,
                        )
                    )


                for box in boxes_local:

                    try:
                        (
                            x0,
                            y0,
                            x1,
                            y1,
                        ) = box

                        p0 = cad_to_view(
                            x0,
                            y0,
                        )

                        p1 = cad_to_view(
                            x1,
                            y1,
                        )

                        if (
                            p0 is None
                            or p1 is None
                        ):
                            continue

                        painter.drawRect(
                            QRect(
                                p0,
                                p1,
                            ).normalized()
                        )

                    except Exception:
                        continue

                painter.end()


        overlay = getattr(
            self,
            "_cad3d_facade_window_overlay",
            None,
        )

        if (
            overlay is None
            or not isinstance(
                overlay,
                QWidget,
            )
        ):
            overlay = (
                _FacadeWindowOverlay(
                    self
                )
            )

            self._cad3d_facade_window_overlay = (
                overlay
            )

            # Parent viewport size changes:
            # normal <-> maximized <-> restored.
            viewport.installEventFilter(
                overlay
            )

            # Canvas changes:
            # zoom / resize / position updates.
            canvas.installEventFilter(
                overlay
            )

            # Scroll and middle-mouse pan eventually modify
            # the scrollbars. Both position AND range changes
            # must refresh the CAD->viewport mapping.
            preview.horizontalScrollBar().valueChanged.connect(
                overlay.sync_to_viewport
            )

            preview.verticalScrollBar().valueChanged.connect(
                overlay.sync_to_viewport
            )

            preview.horizontalScrollBar().rangeChanged.connect(
                overlay.sync_to_viewport
            )

            preview.verticalScrollBar().rangeChanged.connect(
                overlay.sync_to_viewport
            )

        self._cad3d_facade_window_boxes = list(
            boxes
        )

        overlay.sync_to_viewport()

        overlay.show()
        overlay.raise_()
        overlay.update()

        self.status.setText(
            "Cephe Pencereleri | "
            + str(
                len(boxes)
            )
            + " pencere turuncu g\u00f6steriliyor."
        )

        print("")
        print(
            "=== FACADE WINDOW OVERLAY SYNC V2 ==="
        )

        print(
            "WINDOW COUNT:",
            len(boxes),
        )

        print(
            "VIEWPORT:",
            viewport.width(),
            "x",
            viewport.height(),
        )

        print(
            "SCROLL X:",
            preview.horizontalScrollBar().value(),
        )

        print(
            "SCROLL Y:",
            preview.verticalScrollBar().value(),
        )

        print(
            "SELECTION SYSTEM: UNCHANGED"
        )

        print(
            "=== END FACADE WINDOW OVERLAY SYNC V2 ==="
        )

        return len(
            boxes
        )
