    def _on_toggle_facade_windows_clicked(
        self,
        checked,
    ) -> None:

        canvas = self.preview.canvas

        if not bool(
            checked
        ):
            if hasattr(
                canvas,

            ):

            )

            self,
            "current_facade_match_result",
            None,
        )

        if not isinstance(
            result,
            dict,
        ):
            self.facade_windows_button.blockSignals(
                True
            )

            self.facade_windows_button.setChecked(
                False
            )

            self.facade_windows_button.blockSignals(
                False
            )

            self.status.setText(
                "\u00d6nce Cephe Yerle\u015ftir \u00e7al\u0131\u015ft\u0131r."
            )

            return

        for match in (
            result.get(
                "matches",
                [],
            )
            or []
        ):

            for pair in (
                match.get(
                    "opening_pairs",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            ):

                if str(
                    pair.get(
                        "kind",
                        "",
                    )
                    or ""
                ).strip().lower() != "window":
                    continue

                bbox = pair.get(
                    "elevation_bbox"
                )

                if (
                    not isinstance(
                        bbox,
                        (list, tuple),
                    )
                    or len(bbox) != 4
                ):
                    continue

                try:
                    clean = tuple(
                        float(value)
                        for value in bbox
                    )
                except Exception:
                    continue

                key = tuple(
                    round(
                        value,
                        4,
                    )
                    for value in clean
                )

                if key in seen:
                    continue

                seen.add(
                    key
                )

                boxes.append(
                    clean
                )

        if not boxes:

            self.facade_windows_button.blockSignals(
                True
            )

            self.facade_windows_button.setChecked(
                False
            )

            self.facade_windows_button.blockSignals(
                False
            )

            if hasattr(
                canvas,
                "clear_facade_window_highlights",
            ):
                canvas.clear_facade_window_highlights()

            self.status.setText(
                "Cephede e\u015fle\u015fmi\u015f pencere bulunamad\u0131."
            )

            return

        if not hasattr(
            canvas,
            "set_facade_window_highlights",
        ):
            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe pencere overlay sistemi bulunamad\u0131."
            )

            return

        canvas.set_facade_window_highlights(
            boxes
        )

        self.status.setText(
            "Cephe Pencereleri | "
            + str(
                len(
                    boxes
                )
            )
            + " pencere turuncu g\u00f6steriliyor."
        )

--- _cad3d_write_sliding_facade_candidate_audit_v1 | 3188-3825 ---
    def _cad3d_write_sliding_facade_candidate_audit_v1(
        self,
        result,
    ) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Build dimension-relative facade sliding-door candidates.

        AUDIT ONLY:
        - normal swing-door detections stay unchanged
        - window detections stay unchanged
        - Max geometry stays unchanged
        - every candidate still requires plan-gap confirmation
        """

        import json as _sliding_json
        from pathlib import Path as _SlidingPath

        elevation_facades = list(
            result.get(
                "elevation_facades",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        accepted_windows = [
            row
            for row in (
                result.get(
                    "plan_guided_facade_windows",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            )
            if isinstance(
                row,
                dict,
            )
            and str(
                row.get(
                    "kind",
                    "",
                )
                or ""
            ).strip().lower() == "window"
        ]

        candidates = []


        def _bbox_of(
            item,
        ):
            raw = (
                item.get(
                    "bbox"
                )
                or item.get(
                    "elevation_bbox"
                )
            )

            if (
                not isinstance(
                    raw,
                    (list, tuple),
                )
                or len(
                    raw
                ) != 4
            ):
                return None

            try:
                x0, y0, x1, y1 = (
                    float(
                        value
                    )
                    for value in raw
                )

            except (
                TypeError,
                ValueError,
            ):
                return None

            if (
                x1 <= x0
                or y1 <= y0
            ):
                return None

            return [
                x0,
                y0,
                x1,
                y1,
            ]


        for facade in elevation_facades:

            if not isinstance(
                facade,
                dict,
            ):
                continue

            facade_number = (
                facade.get(
                    "facade_number"
                )
            )

            doors = []

            for opening_index, opening in enumerate(
                facade.get(
                    "openings",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            ):

                if not isinstance(
                    opening,
                    dict,
                ):
                    continue

                kind = str(
                    opening.get(
                        "kind",
                        opening.get(
                            "signature_kind",
                            "",
                        ),
                    )
                    or ""
                ).strip().lower()

                if kind != "door":
                    continue

                box = _bbox_of(
                    opening
                )

                if box is None:
                    continue

                doors.append(
                    {
                        "opening_index":
                            opening_index,

                        "bbox":
                            box,

                        "source":
                            opening,
                    }
                )


            doors.sort(
                key=lambda row: (
                    row[
                        "bbox"
                    ][0],
                    row[
                        "bbox"
                    ][1],
                )
            )


            # ================================================
            # GENERIC MULTI-PANEL GROUPING
            #
            # No drawing-specific dimensions.
            #
            # Requirements:
            # - members share practically same bottom/top band
            # - members are directly adjacent
            #
            # Tolerances are proportional to member dimensions.
            # ================================================

            groups = []
            current = []

            for row in doors:

                if not current:
                    current = [
                        row
                    ]
                    continue

                previous = (
                    current[-1]
                )

                a = previous[
                    "bbox"
                ]

                b = row[
                    "bbox"
                ]

                aw = (
                    a[2]
                    - a[0]
                )

                bw = (
                    b[2]
                    - b[0]
                )

                ah = (
                    a[3]
                    - a[1]
                )

                bh = (
                    b[3]
                    - b[1]
                )

                min_height = min(
                    ah,
                    bh,
                )

                min_width = min(
                    aw,
                    bw,
                )

                vertical_tolerance = (
                    min_height
                    * 0.04
                )

                # CAD3D_SLIDING_PANEL_OVERLAP_TOLERANCE_V2
                #
                # Positive gap stays conservative.
                # Negative gap means facade panel bounding boxes
                # overlap, which is common around shared frames.
                #
                # Candidate generation is recall-oriented;
                # plan-gap / swing-door / window confirmation
                # remains the precision filter downstream.
                adjacency_gap_tolerance = (
                    min_width
                    * 0.06
                )

                adjacency_overlap_tolerance = (
                    min_width
                    * 0.10
                )

                same_vertical_band = (
                    abs(
                        a[1]
                        - b[1]
                    )
                    <= vertical_tolerance

                    and

                    abs(
                        a[3]
                        - b[3]
                    )
                    <= vertical_tolerance
                )

                signed_gap = (
                    b[0]
                    - a[2]
                )

                adjacent = (
                    signed_gap
                    <= adjacency_gap_tolerance

                    and

                    signed_gap
                    >= -adjacency_overlap_tolerance
                )

                if (
                    same_vertical_band
                    and adjacent
                ):
                    current.append(
                        row
                    )

                else:

                    if len(
                        current
                    ) >= 2:
                        groups.append(
                            current
                        )

                    current = [
                        row
                    ]


            if len(
                current
            ) >= 2:
                groups.append(
                    current
                )


            # ================================================
            # Use already accepted facade windows only for
            # floor and plan-side context.
            #
            # Sheet absolute coordinates are NOT treated as
            # architectural dimensions.
            # ================================================

            facade_window_rows = []

            for row in accepted_windows:

                try:
                    if int(
                        row.get(
                            "facade_number"
                        )
                    ) != int(
                        facade_number
                    ):
                        continue

                except (
                    TypeError,
                    ValueError,
                ):
                    continue

                center = (
                    row.get(
                        "elevation_center"
                    )
                )

                if (
                    not isinstance(
                        center,
                        (list, tuple),
                    )
                    or len(
                        center
                    ) < 2
                ):
                    continue

                try:
                    center_y = float(
                        center[1]
                    )

                except (
                    TypeError,
                    ValueError,
                ):
                    continue

                facade_window_rows.append(
                    (
                        center_y,
                        row,
                    )
                )


            for group_index, group in enumerate(
                groups,
                start=1,
            ):

                x0 = min(
                    row[
                        "bbox"
                    ][0]
                    for row in group
                )

                y0 = min(
                    row[
                        "bbox"
                    ][1]
                    for row in group
                )

                x1 = max(
                    row[
                        "bbox"
                    ][2]
                    for row in group
                )

                y1 = max(
                    row[
                        "bbox"
                    ][3]
                    for row in group
                )

                center_y = (
                    y0
                    + y1
                ) * 0.5

                floor_order = None
                floor_name = None
                plan_side = None


                if facade_window_rows:

                    _, nearest = min(
                        facade_window_rows,
                        key=lambda item:
                            abs(
                                item[0]
                                - center_y
                            ),
                    )

                    floor_order = (
                        nearest.get(
                            "floor_order"
                        )
                    )

                    floor_name = (
                        nearest.get(
                            "floor_name"
                        )
                    )

                    plan_side = (
                        nearest.get(
                            "plan_side"
                        )
                    )


                members = []

                for row in group:

                    source = (
                        row[
                            "source"
                        ]
                    )

                    members.append(
                        {
                            "opening_index":
                                row[
                                    "opening_index"
                                ],

                            "bbox":
                                row[
                                    "bbox"
                                ],

                            "entity_id":
                                source.get(
                                    "entity_id"
                                ),

                            "source_kind":
                                source.get(
                                    "kind",
                                    source.get(
                                        "signature_kind"
                                    ),
                                ),
                        }
                    )


                candidates.append(
                    {
                        "candidate_id":
                            (
                                "SLIDE_F"
                                + str(
                                    facade_number
                                )
                                + "_"
                                + str(
                                    group_index
                                ).zfill(
                                    2
                                )
                            ),

                        "classification":
                            "sliding_candidate",

                        "facade_number":
                            facade_number,

                        "floor_order":
                            floor_order,

                        "floor_name":
                            floor_name,

                        "plan_side":
                            plan_side,

                        "member_count":
                            len(
                                group
                            ),

                        "elevation_bbox":
                            [
                                x0,
                                y0,
                                x1,
                                y1,
                            ],

                        "elevation_width":
                            (
                                x1
                                - x0
                            ),

                        "elevation_height":
                            (
                                y1
                                - y0
                            ),

                        "members":
                            members,

                        "requires_plan_gap_confirmation":
                            True,

                        "geometry_applied":
                            False,
                    }
                )


        target = (
            _SlidingPath(
                __file__
            )
            .resolve()
            .parents[2]
            / "logs"
            / "sliding_door_facade_candidates.json"
        )

        target.parent.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True,
        )

        payload = {
            "engine":
                "CAD3D_SLIDING_FACADE_CANDIDATE_AUDIT_V1",

            "candidate_count":
                len(
                    candidates
                ),

            "rules":
                {
                    "same_bottom_top_tolerance":
                        "4% of smaller member height",

                    "adjacency_tolerance":
                        "6% of smaller member width",

                    "minimum_member_count":
                        2,

                    "plan_gap_confirmation_required":
                        True,
                },

            "candidates":
                candidates,
        }

        target.write_text(
            _sliding_json.dumps(
                payload,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=2,
            ),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        print(
            "SLIDING FACADE CANDIDATE AUDIT V1 | CANDIDATES:",
            len(
                candidates
            ),
        )

        print(
            "SLIDING CANDIDATE AUDIT:",
            str(
                target
            ),
        )

        return candidates

--- _cad3d_run_facade_analysis_silent_v1 | 3830-3924 ---
    def _cad3d_run_facade_analysis_silent_v1(
        self,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Run the existing facade analysis automatically when
        Plan Alani Sec is clicked.

        The old facade-window overlay is not displayed.
        """

        file_type = str(
            getattr(
                self,
                "selected_file_type",
                "",
            )
            or ""
        ).lower()

        if file_type not in {
            ".dwg",
            ".dxf",
        }:
            return True


        try:
            from src.cad.facade_window_button_engine import (
                run_facade_window_button,
            )

            result = (
                run_facade_window_button(
                    self
                )
            )


            from src.cad.main_entrance_door_runtime import (
                resolve_main_entrance_door,
            )

            result = (
                resolve_main_entrance_door(
                    self,
                    result,
                )
            )


            self.current_facade_match_result = (
                result
            )


            # No visual facade-window inspection in the new flow.
            self._cad3d_hide_facade_window_overlay()


            # Sliding candidates are audit/data only at this stage.
            self._cad3d_write_sliding_facade_candidate_audit_v1(
                result
            )


            count = len(
                result.get(
                    "plan_guided_facade_windows",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            )


            print(
                "AUTO FACADE ANALYSIS ON PLAN SELECT V1 | WINDOWS:",
                count,
            )


            return True


        except Exception as exc:

            self._cad3d_hide_facade_window_overlay()

            print(
                "AUTO FACADE ANALYSIS WARNING:",
                repr(
                    exc
                ),
            )

            return False

--- _cad3d_facade_windows_preselection_clicked | 3927-4002 ---
    def _cad3d_facade_windows_preselection_clicked(
        self,
        *_args,
    ) -> None:
        """
        CAD3D_FACADE_WINDOW_BUTTON_ISOLATED_V1

        ONLY this button workflow.

        Does not invoke:
            floor selection
            floor confirmation
            floor assignment
            multi-floor 3D prepare
            Max sender
            3D create
        """

        try:
            from src.cad.facade_window_button_engine import (
                run_facade_window_button,
            )

            result = (
                run_facade_window_button(
                    self
                )
            )
            # CAD3D_MAIN_ENTRANCE_PLAN_FIRST_BINDING_V1
            from src.cad.main_entrance_door_runtime import (
                resolve_main_entrance_door,
            )
            result = resolve_main_entrance_door(
                self,
                result,
            )

        except Exception as exc:

            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe pencere analizi yapilamadi: "
                + str(
                    exc
                )
            )

            print(
                "CEPHE PENCERELERI BUTTON FAILED:",
                repr(
                    exc
                ),
            )

            return


        self.current_facade_match_result = (
            result
        )


        self._cad3d_show_facade_window_overlay()


        count = len(
            result.get(
                "plan_guided_facade_windows",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )


        self.status.setText(
            f"Cephe Pencereleri | {count} pencere"
        )

--- _cad3d_lock_facade_windows_after_plan_selection | 4005-4042 ---
    def _cad3d_lock_facade_windows_after_plan_selection(
        self,
        *_args,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Visual facade inspection must never alter the viewport
        after floor-plan selection has started.
        """

        self._cad3d_hide_facade_window_overlay()

        button = getattr(
            self,
            "facade_windows_button",
            None,
        )

        if button is None:
            return

        try:
            button.blockSignals(
                True
            )

            if button.isCheckable():
                button.setChecked(
                    False
                )

            button.setEnabled(
                False
            )

        finally:
            button.blockSignals(
                False
            )

--- _on_place_facades_clicked | 4044-4343 ---
    def _on_place_facades_clicked(
        self,
    ) -> None:
        import json


        # Reset old facade-window visualization.
        if hasattr(
            self,
            "facade_windows_button",
        ):
            self.facade_windows_button.blockSignals(
                True
            )

            self.facade_windows_button.setChecked(
                False
            )

            self.facade_windows_button.setEnabled(
                False
            )

            self.facade_windows_button.blockSignals(
                False
            )

        if hasattr(
            self.preview.canvas,
            "clear_facade_window_highlights",
        ):
            self.preview.canvas.clear_facade_window_highlights()

        from src.cad.facade_matcher import (
            auto_analyse_dwg_facades,
        )

        file_type = str(
            getattr(
                self,
                "selected_file_type",
                "",
            )
            or ""
        ).lower()

        if file_type not in {
            ".dwg",
            ".dxf",
        }:
            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe Yerle\u015ftir | DWG/DXF a\u00e7."
            )
            return

        cad_data = getattr(
            self,
            "cad_data",
            None,
        )

        if not isinstance(
            cad_data,
            dict,
        ):
            cad_data = getattr(
                self.preview,
                "cad_data",
                None,
            )

        if not isinstance(
            cad_data,
            dict,
        ):
            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe Yerle\u015ftir | DWG verisi yok."
            )
            return

        full_geometry = list(
            cad_data.get(
                "geometry",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        if not full_geometry:
            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe Yerle\u015ftir | DWG geometrisi bo\u015f."
            )
            return

        try:
            result = (
                auto_analyse_dwg_facades(
                    full_geometry
                )
            )

            # CAD3D_FACADE_PRESELECTION_RAW_ONLY_V3
            #
            # At this stage floor packages do NOT exist yet.
            # Therefore no plan-guided acceptance and no vertical
            # measurements are allowed here.
            #
            # This result is inspection/candidate data only.
            result[
                "facade_analysis_stage"
            ] = "RAW_PRESELECTION_ONLY"

            result[
                "plan_guided_applied"
            ] = False

            self._cad3d_raw_facade_match_result = result

        except Exception as exc:
            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe Yerle\u015ftir | Analiz hatas\u0131: "
                + str(exc)
            )
            return

        log_path = (
            PROJECT_ROOT
            / "logs"
            / "facade_match_runtime.json"
        )

        log_path.parent.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True,
        )

        log_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(
                result,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=2,
                default=str,
            ),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        main_plan = result.get(
            "main_plan"
        )

        facades = list(
            result.get(
                "elevation_facades",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        matches = list(
            result.get(
                "matches",
                [],
            )
            or []
        )

        if not main_plan:
            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe Yerle\u015ftir | Ana plan otomatik bulunamad\u0131."
            )
            return

        if not facades:
            self.status.setText(
                "Cephe Yerle\u015ftir | Ana plan bulundu fakat cephe bulunamad\u0131."
            )
            return

        full_bounds = result.get(
            "full_bounds"
        )

        canvas = (
            self.preview.canvas
        )

        canvas.set_cad_scene(
            full_geometry,
            tuple(
                float(value)
                for value in full_bounds
            ),
            hidden_layers=set(),
            mode="cad",
        )

        labels = []

        for facade in facades:
            labels.append(
                {
                    "number":
                        int(
                            facade[
                                "facade_number"
                            ]
                        ),

                    "point":
                        tuple(
                            facade[
                                "anchor"
                            ]
                        ),
                }
            )

        for match in matches:
            labels.append(
                {
                    "number":
                        int(
                            match[
                                "facade_number"
                            ]
                        ),

                    "point":
                        tuple(
                            match[
                                "plan_anchor"
                            ]
                        ),
                }
            )

        if hasattr(
            canvas,
            "set_facade_match_labels",
        ):
            canvas.set_facade_match_labels(
                labels
            )

        self.current_facade_match_result = (
            result
        )


        _facade_matched_window_count = sum(
            1
            for _match in (
                result.get(
                    "matches",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            )
            for _pair in (
                _match.get(
                    "opening_pairs",
                    [],
                )
                or []
            )
            if (
                str(
                    _pair.get(
                        "kind",
                        "",
                    )
                    or ""
                ).strip().lower()
                == "window"
                and isinstance(
                    _pair.get(
                        "elevation_bbox"
                    ),
                    (list, tuple),
                )
                and len(
                    _pair.get(
                        "elevation_bbox"
                    )
                )
                == 4
            )
        )

        self.facade_windows_button.setEnabled(
            _facade_matched_window_count > 0
        )

        self.status.setText(
            "Cephe Yerle\u015ftir | "
            + str(len(facades))
            + " cephe bulundu | "
            + str(len(matches))
            + " plan kenari eslesti."
        )

--- _cad3d_find_facade_button | 10744-10779 ---
def _cad3d_find_facade_button(root):
    stack = list(
        root.children()
    )

    while stack:
        obj = stack.pop(0)

        try:
            text_value = obj.text()
        except Exception:
            text_value = None

        if text_value is not None:
            normalized = (
                _cad3d_norm_button_text(
                    text_value
                )
            )

            if (
                "cephe"
                in normalized
                and "yerle\u015ftir"
                in normalized
            ):
                return obj

        try:
            stack.extend(
                obj.children()
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    return None
